Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The engineering variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 339 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 445 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the engineering decomposition. By minimality, the element a^339 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 445-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
